out-code-review-p1#1	DEFER severity=LOW — src/tests/surfaces/failure-state-declared.logic.test.ts:44 "const ea = d.errors?.answer ?? 'none';" — folds an absent `errors` block into the literal `'none'`, so the emitted line "its errors answer is none" can be false when no `errors` key exists at all; conflates "declared none" with "not declared" — check `d.errors?.answer` presence with a distinct message, e.g. "declares no errors answer".
out-code-review-p1#2	DEFER severity=LOW — src/tests/surfaces/nav-tab-switch.logic.test.ts:118 "expect(files.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(20);" — a hard-coded floor (also `toBeLessThanOrEqual(2)` on ALLOW and `21 - ...` in handbook-rule-map C-T3) breaks silently the moment the mobile file set legitimately grows or shrinks; move the expected minimum to the descriptor manifest or derive it.
out-code-review-p1#3	DEFER severity=LOW — src/tests/surfaces/nav-tab-switch.logic.test.ts:96 "const routes = new Set(tabRoutes(TAB_SOURCE, readFileSync(join(ROOT, TAB_SOURCE), 'utf8')).values());" — runs at module load, so a reshaped `PARCEL_TAB_ROUTE` throws during collection and the named test B2 ("a reshaped tab constant fails closed") never reports its regex message; the fail-closed behaviour exists but its assertion is dead in that scenario, so build `routes` lazily or inside the tests that need it.
out-code-review-p1#4	DEFER severity=NIT — src/tests/surfaces/nav-tab-switch.logic.test.ts:105 "for (const p of [d.identity?.route, ...(d['x-draft']?.files ?? [])]) if (p && p.startsWith('mobile/')" — `identity.route` is a URL route, not a file path, so it can never satisfy `startsWith('mobile/')`; that half of the array is dead code against the stated file filter — drop `d.identity?.route` or document why a descriptor could carry a `mobile/` path there.
out-code-review-p2#1	BUG severity=HIGH — src/tests/surfaces/scope-fence.logic.test.ts:69-70 "if (screen) { hit(n, `lead screen import: ${screen} from ${JSON.stringify(n.moduleSpecifier.text)}`); return; }" / "return; // the module path is checked above; its string is not a route or a table read" — the unconditional return skips the whole import subtree, so rule (1)'s identifier form is never checked on any import binding: "import lead_parcels from './db';" and "import { LEAD_PARCELS } from './db';" produce zero hits because names are screened only against LEAD_SCREENS, and line 74 never runs for them — fix: run the LEAD_TABLE_RE test over the `names` array (and over the matched importClause identifiers) before returning, and add both forms to test B.
out-code-review-p2#2	BUG severity=MED — src/tests/surfaces/scope-fence.logic.test.ts:60 "if ((ts.isImportDeclaration(n) || ts.isExportDeclaration(n)) && n.moduleSpecifier && ts.isStringLiteral(n.moduleSpecifier)) {" — an export without a module specifier never enters the branch, so "export { LeadFeed };" is walked as plain identifiers and tested only against LEAD_TABLE_RE, giving 0 hits although rule (2) covers exports that name a lead screen; namespace imports are also dropped by "if (c.namedBindings && ts.isNamedImports(c.namedBindings))" (line 65) — fix: match the binding names against LEAD_SCREENS outside the moduleSpecifier guard and add a NamespaceImport case.
out-code-review-p2#3	DEFER severity=MED — src/tests/surfaces/scope-fence.logic.test.ts:87 "const name = typeof t === 'string' ? t : t.table ?? '';" — an unknown table-ref shape falls back to '' and the descriptor check silently passes instead of failing, so a ref written as {name: 'lead_parcels'} reads as clean; the same line throws an unlabelled TypeError if tables is an object rather than an array — throw with the descriptor path when a table ref yields neither a string nor t.table.
out-code-review-p2#4	DEFER severity=MED — src/tests/surfaces/scope-fence.logic.test.ts:45 "const scope = (JSON.parse(readText(MANIFEST)) as { scope: { programme_scopes: string[] } }).scope;" — parsed once at module scope with an unchecked cast, so a missing or renamed manifest key throws at import time and takes tests B and C (which are pure and need no manifest) down with it — read and parse inside test A, and assert programme_scopes is an array before use.
out-code-review-p2#5	DEFER severity=MED — src/tests/surfaces/scope-fence.logic.test.ts:100 ".map((f) => ({ path: f, d: JSON.parse(readText(f)) as Descriptor }))" — a malformed descriptor throws a bare SyntaxError that names no file, which is the one thing this gate needs in its failure output — wrap the parse and rethrow with f in the message.
out-code-review-p2#6	DEFER severity=MED — src/tests/surfaces/scope-fence.logic.test.ts:74 "else if (ts.isIdentifier(n) && LEAD_TABLE_RE.test(n.text)) hit(n, `lead_parcels identifier: ${n.text}`);" — no planted case in test B ever reaches this branch (the five cases are two strings, two strings, one string plus one import-name), and none exercises the export path, so both can regress while the suite stays green — add "const t = lead_parcels;" and "export { LeadFeed };" to the cases array.
out-code-review-p2#7	DEFER severity=LOW — src/tests/surfaces/scope-fence.logic.test.ts:35-36 "kind?: string;" / "identity?: { id?: string; route?: string };" — kind and identity.id are declared but never read anywhere in the file (only identity.route at line 108), dead surface introduced by the diff — drop the two fields or use them.

out-error-paths-p1#3	BUG severity=MED — src/tests/surfaces/failure-state-declared.logic.test.ts:51 "return execFileSync('git', ['ls-files', '--', 'scripts/surfaces/'], { cwd: ROOT, stdio: 'pipe' })" (same shape in scope-fence.logic.test.ts and nav-tab-switch.logic.test.ts, and handbook-rule-map.logic.test.ts) — no timeout: execFileSync blocks the event loop, so a hung git (index.lock, stale mount) hangs the suite instead of failing a test, the vitest timeout never fires — fix: pass { timeout: 30000, killSignal: 'SIGKILL' } and let the throw fail the test.
